[LAUGHTER] 28. - Joey - . I wonder if they can hear me talk or like feel the vibrations in the sand when we move around. 29. - Fey - . I'm cold. 30. - Joey - . Do you want my jacket? 31. - Fey - . It's after eleven, let's go. 32. - Joey - . What?  We just got here.  What's the point of coming all the way down here if we just turn around and go home? 33. - Fey - . There is no point. 34. - Joey - . Right. 35. - Fey - . That's my point. 36. - Joey - . Wait, that's my point, too. 37. - Fey - . Well, then let's go. 38. - Joey - . No, no.  Okay.  That's not my point.  My point is there is no point in coming down here if we just turn around and go home.  Don't you want to see it? 39. - Fey - . Not particularly. 40. - Joey - . Why not? 41. - Fey - . I just don't.  It's stupid. 42. - Joey - . Honey, this is like a natural phenomenon. I mean... People in Kansas would have to get plane reservations and get hotel rooms to see this.  It's just forty minutes away for us; it's too good to pass up. 43. - Fey - . Pass what up?  I mean these fish swim out of the ocean, they flop around on the beach, they swim away and then they die. 44. - Joey - . Yeah.  But this..this moment on the beach is like the highlight of their little lives.  All that flopping around is laying eggs or what to do - spawning or mating or whatever. 45. - Joey - . This is a big night for these little guys.  It's like life's last orgy. 46. - Fey - . I don't see why you're so excited; this isn't a freaking moon landing we are talking about.  It's fish.  It's a fish nobody even eats. 47. - Joey - . Yeah they do. 48. - Fey - . Oh Augie 49. - Joey - . okay... well, Whatever.  But it's a fish who do something no other fish do.  It's like those swallows returning to Capistrano or like those lemmings that throw themselves off the cliffs. 50. - Joey - . There's like mystery here, there's magic.  It's like a little bit of the unexplainable.  I just can't see how you're not interested. 51. - Fey - . I'm just not. 52. - Joey - . For real? 53. - Fey - . Yeah. 54. - Joey - . God, I don't get it.  you know the first time we came here, you said it was the best night of your life? 55. - Joey - . And last year, I remember distinctly you said, you were so excited to get here that you don't remember you stubbed your toe until we were in the car. 56. - Fey - . Last time, you'll also remember, we got our shoes wet, I got sand in my panties, you got a sore throat and we got into a big fight and we did not see the grunions. 57. - Joey - . Ahh...we weren't in a good spot. 58. - Fey - . Hmm.  We didn't see them the year before either. 59. - Joey - . We just weren't in a good spot.  I'm telling you, this is a much better spot.  I have instincts for these things. 60. - Joey - . Look there, see?  What's that?  No, that's - that's just seaweed. 61. - Joey - . God, this is great.  Think about this.  I know you are not interested, but just think about this. 62. - Joey - . Right now out there, there is this mass of silver fish and it's heading this way.  No one sent them an invitation, no one told them, no one even gave them a map. 63. - Joey - . Just all at once, they turned and they formed this line as long as the California coast.  And some instinctual little firecracker went off in their bodies and they all just turned as one and started swimming. 64. - Joey - . They are swimming right now.  They are swimming as if that is the only thing that matters to them. 65. - Joey - . It's single mindedness with no mind.  It is urgency that is just pure urge. 66. - Joey - . And they are swimming right now to this very point where we stand for this huge celebration of sex and death. 67. - Joey - . Oh look.  I'm giving myself goose bumps, my hairs are standing up on my arm. 68. - Fey - . That's the wind. 69. - Joey - . So what do you think? 70. - Fey - . About what? 71. - Joey - . About all of that, about all I was talking about.

1.  - Joey - Look at this, all of the hairs are standing up on my arm. 2. - Fey - . That's the wind. 3. - Joey - . Well, so, what do you think? 4. - Fey - . About what? 5. - Joey - . About what I was saying. 6. - Fey - . I think it's ridiculous. 7. - Joey - . It certainly is not.  It's slightly exaggerated scientific fact. 8. - Fey - . It's PR! Somebody sold you a bunch of crap that there is this wonderful thing that is going to happen tonight on the beach. 9. - Joey - . Not just me, look at all of these people. 10. - Fey - . Everybody's told the same story.  It keeps us coming back for more, it keeps us excited.  We keep thinking that life is going start at any minute if we can just find the right spot, get in on the action. 11. - Joey - . Are we talking about the same thing, here? 12. - Fey - . I'm sorry, it's just fish to me. 13. - Joey - . Carla, can I ask you something?  Now, don't take this the wrong way, but are you on your period? 14. - Fey - . Don't ask me that.  I hate it when you ask me that, you always ask me that.  It's insulting. 15. - Joey - . Well then, what's bugging you? 16. - Fey - . We just have different ideas of what this night is supposed to look like. 17. - Joey - . There is nothing to it; we come down here, the fish arrive, they flop around, they do their fish thing.  We go ooh, ah, look at the fish and we go home. 18. - Fey - . For God- Grow up, Augie.  The fish are not going to arrive. 19. - Joey - . Why not? 20. - Fey - . Because they never do.  Have they...Have they ever? 21. - Joey - . We've missed them twice. 22. - Fey - . Twice is every time we have tried; that's ever. 23. - Joey - . They'll show up this time. 24. - Fey - . No they won't.  It's like waiting for Santa Claus. 25. - Fey - . I feel like my whole life is going to be spent standing on this beach with my eyes wide open and my hands clasped expectantly waiting for fish to show up.  And the fish never show up. 26. - Joey - . I thought you wanted to see it. 27. - Fey - . You don't understand anything I am saying. 28. - Joey - . No. Of course not. 29. - Fey - . I did want to see it, I still do, I always do.  I just... 30. - Fey - . I feel like I'm always just standing here waiting.  I feel like this night is going to be the night, but it never is. 31. - Joey - . It's just fish, it's nothing to get traumatized over. 32. - Fey - . For God's sake, this is not about fish. 33. - Joey - . Okay.  I'm trying to work this backwards and pinpoint the exact moment where we took a turn for the worst here. 34. - Joey - . Okay.  So you were fine during that phone call from Marge and then dinner was okay.  And doing the dishes was fine and then I was going to take a shower and you're watching the news and you said the grunions are running tonight, do you want to go?  And I said, sure great, let's go. 35. - Joey - . Okay.  So I am putting out the pets, getting the car out of the garage. 36. - Fey - . Do you remember the first year we came here?  It was like four years after we got married. 37. - Fey - . We thought I was pregnant, we got a bottle of champagne, we had no glasses.  We took off our shoes and you asked me to dance. 38. - Fey - . You whispered the sweetest, most intimate things to me right into my ear so I could feel them as much as hear them.  And I remember thinking, this is it.  You know, finally, I am as happy as I'm supposed to be. 39. - Joey - . Wait a minute, I got it. 40. - Fey - . Drinking champagne from the bottle, 41. - Joey - . It's when we were driving past that Knickerbocker Liquor Locker, you said, let's get some champagne.  I said no, time's a wasting or something like that. 42. - Fey - . dancing barefoot in the sand. 43. - Joey - . That's what it is?  Right?  You want this to be like the other time, right? 44. - Fey - . I'd rather not remember some things.  I'd rather not hope for some things. 45. - Joey - . Sure.  All right.  Okay. 46. - Joey - . Listen, do you want to dance? 47. - Fey - . No. 48. - Joey - . Do you want me to go get some champagne? 49. - Fey - . No. 50. - Joey - . Do you want to take off your shoes? 51. - Fey - . No, no. Nothing. 52. - Joey - . Then what? 53. - Joey - . Do you want to get married again? 54. - Fey - . No. 55. - Joey - . What?  What?  Do you want a divorce? 56. - Joey - . A vacation? 57. - Fey - . No. 58. - Joey - . Like a new carpet?  A poodle?  A back rub?  Ice cream?  A suicide pact?  What do you want?  What the hell do you want? 59. - Fey - . I just want something to turn out like I expected it to. 60. - Joey - . Well welcome to the human race, huh?  Do you think I had some grand fantasy going that four years down the line we would come to this beach and be arguing over fish?  No. 61. - Fey - . [BREATHING] 62. - Joey - . No I'm just like you.  I thought it was going to be all roses and peaches and the bills would just pay themselves.  And we'll have three, fat rosy cheeked children.  Huh, baby, the joke's on us.  Because you know what you get? 63. - Joey - . Do you know what you get?  You get this. 64. - Fey - . This? What is this?  This is nothing.  This isn't even anything. 65. - Joey - . Yes it is. 66. - Fey - . Sure.  This is standing on the beach waiting.  This is fighting. 67. - Joey - . Right 68. - Fey - . I mean this is a lot of things, but it's just... 69. - Joey - . [BREATHING] 70. - Fey - . Do you understand what I am saying? 71. - Joey - . I'm doing the best I can. 72. - Fey - . I know you are Augie, I know. 73. - Fey - . I know you meant this to be special, I know that you wanted it to be right, but I just can't help thinking that, I can't help wishing that I was somewhere else. 74. - Joey - . Maybe if you were with someone else, too. 75. - Fey - . I didn't say that. 76. - Joey - . I just- I know I don't make you happy. 77. - Fey - . Don't be ridiculous.  Anything that- anything I am doing, I'd rather be doing it with you. 78. - Joey - . Yeah? 79. - Fey - . Yeah.  Don't you know that? 80. - Fey - . For Heaven's sake Augie. 81. - Fey - . You're the one who probably wishes he was with somebody else.  Somebody who doesn't take things so seriously, who could enjoy herself. 82. - Joey - . You know, actually now that you mention it, no I don't. 83. - Fey - . So maybe we are in the wrong spot, but we are with the right person. 84. - Joey - . I don't know, it seemed like such a good spot to me. 85. - Joey - . We have a great view of the moon from here, though, huh? 86. - Fey - . Hmm.  I noticed that, it's pretty. Big old white moon. 87. - Joey - . [BREATHING] 88. - Joey - . And look what I got here. 89. - Fey - . Augie, you bought refreshments. 90. - Joey - . It's not champagne. 91. - Fey - . I guess we don't need glasses. 92. - Joey - . [BREATHING] 93. - Joey - . Do you want to go home?  Are you cold? 94. - Fey - . I'm beginning to think this might be the right spot after all. 95. - Fey - . Augie, I'm sorry. 96. - Joey - . Shh.  If we're very quiet, the fish might come. 97. - Fey - . Or not. 98. - Joey - . Or not.

Do they? 20. - Fey - . Hmm.  The pity is that one can't stay happy. 21. - Joey - . Darling, don't say that. 22. - Fey - . It's true.  I think the whole thing's a cruel joke. 23. - Joey - . What?  This beautiful and sacred thing - love? 24. - Fey - . Yes.  Meaning just that. 25. - Joey - . Well what does it all mean?  That's what I ask myself in my quest for ultimate truth.  Dear God, what does it all mean? 26. - Fey - . Don't laugh at me.  I'm being serious. 27. - Joey - . Oh darling, you mustn't be serious.  It's just what they want. 28. - Fey - . Who's they? 29. - Joey - . All of those futile moralists who make life unbearable.  You have to laugh at them, be flippant. 30. - Joey - . We are figures of fun.  All right. 33. - Fey - . How long with it last, this ludicrous overbearing love of ours? 34. - Joey - . Who knows? 35. - Fey - . Well it depends on how well we've played. 39. - Fey - . What happens if one of us dies?  Does the other one still laugh? 40. - Joey - . Yes.  Yes with all of his might. 41. - Fey - . Well that's serious enough, isn't it? 42. - Joey - . No, no, no, no. no.  Death is a very laughable thing.  It's a cunning little mystery, it's all done with mirrors. 43. - Fey - . Let's blow trumpets and squeakers and enjoy the party for as long as we can, you know, like small, idiotic school children. 46. - Fey - . [LAUGHTER] 47. - Joey - . Oh Come and kiss me darling before your body rots and worms pop out of your eye sockets. 48. - Fey - . Oh Elliott, worms don't pop. 49. - Joey - . Oh See, I don't care what you do, see.  You can paint yourself green and run naked through the Place Vendome and run off with all of the men of the world.  I shan't say a word, just as long as you love me best. 50. - Fey - . Aw.  Thank you, darling.  The same applies to you too, except that if you so much as look at another woman I'll kill you. 51. - Joey - . Do you remember that awful scene we had in Venice? 52. - Fey - . Which particular one? 53. - Joey - . The one where you put that tiny painted wooden snake in my bed. 54. - Fey - . Oh Charles, that was his name.  He did wiggle so beautifully. 55. - Joey - . I don't see how you cannot be interested. 47. - Fey - . I'm just not. 48. - Joey - . For real? 49. - Fey - . Yes. 50. - Joey - . I don't get it. The first time we came here you said it was the best night of your life. 51. - Joey - . We weren't in a good spot. 54. - Fey - . We didn't see them the first time either. 55. - Joey - . We just weren't in a good spot; this is a much better spot. I've got good instincts for these things. 56. - Joey - . There is that, oh no that's just seaweed, isn't it? 57. - Joey - . This is, this is great, isn't it? I wouldn't miss this for anything. Now listen to this, I know you're not interested but just listen. 58. - Joey - . Out there somewhere there is this huge mass of silver fish headed this way, 59. - Joey - . they don't why, we don't know why. They didn't take a vote or anything; they didn't get a map or an invitation. 60. - Joey - . Just all as one. This little time sensitive firecracker, instinctual firecracker in their body just went off and all as one they turned and formed a line as long the California coast and started swimming. They're swimming right now as if that's the only thing that matters. 61. - Joey - . It's single mindedness, with no mind, it's urgency that's pure urge, they don't know what drives them. What I was saying. 68. - Fey - . It's ridiculous.

1.  - Joey - Hey, what time is it?  This pokes around midnight right?  God this is great isn't it? 2. - Joey - . Look at the night we got.  I wouldn't trade this for anything. 3. - Joey - . Is that, is that, nah, is that just Paul?  I can't even tell. 4. - Joey - . But if I can't tell, it probably isn't them isn't it?  Probably would be unmistakable wouldn't you think?  How are you doing? 5. - Joey - . Oh, you know what I forgot?  I forgot my flashlight.  How could I be so stupid?  I should've brought my flashlight. 6. - Fey - . What flashlight? 7. - Joey - . You know, the flashlight, the silver one?  There's only one isn't there? 8. - Fey - . It's not your flashlight. 9. - Joey - . Well, whose is it? 10. - Fey - . It's ours.  Mine too. 11. - Joey - . No.  Of course, our flashlight.  Yours and mine.  Me flashlight is su flashlight, naturally. 12. - Joey - . How could we have not thought to bring it?  I hope the moon stays out. 13. - Joey - . Was it a full moon last year when they were running?  I can't remember.  Do you remember? 14. - Fey - . Yes. 15. - Joey - . Are you cold?  Do you want my jacket? 16. - Joey - . We should've brought the blanket, our blanket. 17. - Joey - . God.  Oh, you know what would be good right about now?  A six-pack.  A six-pack would be just the ticket. 18. - Joey - . I'm so stupid, I should've brought a six-pack.  God, I'm like a little kid.  It's amazing I get out of the house with my fly zipped up. Oh- [LAUGHTER] 19. - Joey - . I wonder if they can hear us talk?  Or feel the vibrations in the sand when we move around? 20. - Fey - . Look, I'm cold. 21. - Joey - . Do you want my jacket? 22. - Fey - . It's past eleven, let's go. 23. - Joey - . What?  Are you kidding me?  We just got here.  We miss it.  What's the point of coming down here if we just turn around and go home? 24. - Fey - . There is no point in coming down here. 25. - Joey - . Right. 26. - Fey - . That's my point. 27. - Joey - . That's my point too...No.  No.  That's not my point.  My point is what's the point if we come down here if we just turn around and leave? 28. - Fey - . So let's go. 29. - Joey - . Why not? 30. - Fey - . I don't know.  I just don't. 31. - Joey - . Honey, this is a natural phenomenon.  It only happens once a year.  I mean think about the people in Kansas, they would have to get hotel reservations and plane tickets to come and see this.  We're only forty minutes away.  This is too good to pass up. 32. - Fey - . Pass up what?  These fish swim up to shore, on the beach and then they flop around a little, they swim away and then they die. 33. - Joey - . Well this is like the highlight, the one night of their lives and that flopping around, that's laying eggs or spawning or mating or whatever you call it. 34. - Joey - . But this is the big night for these guys.  Their last, life's last orgy. 35. - Fey - . What are you getting so excited about?  I mean, it's not like it's like the landing on the moon or something.  I mean, it's just fish, fish that nobody eats. 36. - Joey - . Yeah they do. 37. - Fey - . Augie. 38. - Joey - . Okay.  But it's fish that do something that no other fish do.  Like the swallows in Capistrano or the Lemmings that throw themselves off the cliffs, there's magic here and some mystery and a little bit of unexplainable. 39. - Joey - . I mean, I can't see how you can not be interested. 40. - Fey - . I'm just not. 41. - Joey - . For real? 42. - Fey - . Yes. 43. - Joey - . I don't get it.  I mean the first time we came to see it, you said it was the best night of your life. 44. - Joey - . And last year when we came you ran out of the house so fast you stubbed your toe and you didn't even notice it until we got in the car. 45. - Fey - . You also could remember that I got sand in my panties, we got in an argument over nothing, you got a sore throat and we didn't even get to see the Grunions. 46. - Joey - . Okay.  We're in a good spot. 47. - Fey - . You didn't see them the first time either. 48. - Joey - . We just weren't in the good spot.  This is a much better spot.  You know, I have a good instincts for these things. 49. - Joey - . Look, is that...no is that just seaweed? 50. - Joey - . You know, this is great isn't it?  You know, I wouldn't trade this for anything. 51. - Joey - . I mean, think about this, I know you're not interested but just think about this for a minute. 52. - Joey - . Somewhere out there, there's this huge silver mass of fish heading this way, they don't why, we don't know why, they just some little instinctual fire cracker went off in their bodies... 53. - Joey - . ...like and they all just turned at once and formed a line as long as the California coast and started swimming this way.  They didn't, you know, they didn't make a decision, they didn't, you know, have boats or anything.  No one sent them an invitation or a map, they just started swimming as if that's the only thing that matters. 54. - Joey - . It's single mindedness with no mind.  It's urgency that's pure urge and they don't, they don't ask it a question, they don't give it a name, they just swim. 55. - Joey - . They just start swimming to this place right here where we stand for massive celebration of sex and death. 56. - Joey - . Oh, I'm giving myself goose bumps.  Look, all my hairs are standing up. 57. - Fey - . That's the wind. 58. - Joey - . [BREATHING] So what do you think? 59. - Fey - . About what? 60. - Joey - . About what I just said?
